## Demo: pool switch in tab 6 (19-10-2026)

Decision: tab 6 (app/streamlit_app.py) shows one mixed artist list: the genre filter and the artist select list the artists of both pools together, sorted by name, and no pool selector is on screen. The pool is used only inside the code to pick the files (pool_b.pool_lists and pool_b.paths of app/demo_config.yaml); the selector texts and default pool were removed from the config. The genre and artist selectors have one widget key each, the game history is kept per artist id, and the session total includes both pools. The rest of tab 6 is unchanged (entry "Demo: tab 6 flow and scoring (19-10-2026)").
Data layer: src/app_live.py load_tables concatenates the pool A file and the pool B file of each table, pool A rows first and unchanged, with the pool B paths read from pool_b.paths: works (results/demo/works_demo.csv and works_demo_b.csv), WHEN features (results/demo/live/when_features.csv and when_features_b.csv), WHEN scores (results/demo/when_demo_scores.csv and when_demo_scores_b.csv), WHAT features (results/demo/live/what_features_all_years.csv and what_features_b.csv) and WHAT labels (results/demo/live/what_labels.csv and what_labels_b.csv). baseline_c_score comes only from results/holdout_what/row_scores.csv and stays empty for pool B. score_when, score_what and artist_timeline are unchanged in use; score_what returns an empty table for an artist without a WHAT score (boygenius).
Proof of concept marks: for a reality in the year not over (2026, entry "Counting rule by reality year and the 2027 forecast (20-10-2026)") the result is shown as the real result in its normal color with an asterisk (a green check with an asterisk when the prediction equals the reality, a red cross with an asterisk when not) and no longer as an hourglass mark; the last data year stays grey. On tab 7 the reason of each flagged year explains the asterisk (style_tab.reasons); tab 6 uses poc.texts.legend_open_year.
Test results (print-only scripts, 19-10-2026, repeated after the removal of the selector on 19-10-2026 with the same pool A and pool B outputs, no pool widget on the page and an artist list sorted by name): the WHEN scores of pool B equal results/demo/when_demo_scores_b.csv (740 rows matched, none unmatched, maximum difference 2.98e-08, limit 0.00005); the WHAT scores of pool B exist only for the 117 rows of what_features_b.csv, with labels and flags equal to what_labels_b.csv and baseline_c_score empty; the pool A scoreboards (7592689: 5 counted, 3 model right; 1124645 and 4778: 6 counted, 6 model right) and the WHAT scores of 1124645 in 2020 and 7592689 in 2023 equal the earlier runs, and the tab 6 AppTest output for pool A is identical to the earlier run except for the timings; Kendrick Lamar, Beyonce, Peso Pluma (WHAT only from 2023) and boygenius (3 playable years, no WHAT score in any year, the WHAT column shows game.what_missing) run without exception in tab 6 and in the proof of concept.
Limitations: the pool B artists were not checked for display by hand.

## Demo: route table for choosing a disclosed route (19-10-2026)

Files: src/route_table.py (logic) and scripts/run_demo_route_table.py (calls it; refuses to run when the output exists and writes with exclusive creation). Output: results/demo/route_table.csv, 32.4 KB, 430 rows (307 of pool A and 123 of pool B), one row per artist and playable year from 2018, with the scores rounded to 4 decimals. Columns: artist, artist_id, pool, year, when_score, when_verdict, when_reality, when_counted, when_right, what_available, what_score, what_verdict, what_reality, what_label_after_holdout, what_right.
Rules: nothing is refitted and nothing is evaluated anew; the values are the live scores and stored labels that tab 6 shows (src/app_live.py artist_timeline). The WHEN verdict is Yes at a score of at least game.threshold (0.5); the WHEN reality counts under the rule of is_counted of src/demo_game.py (label present, not is_projection, not label_after_holdout). The WHAT verdict is the one of tab 6: Yes at a score at or above score_cutoff of results/holdout_what/top10_lift.csv (the top 10% rule of the entries "WHAT threshold-metric report" and "WHAT final holdout evaluation of stage 0"), not 0.5; the WHAT reality is the stored label, flagged or not.
Purpose: the table is for choosing a disclosed demo route, not for evaluation. It was built after the scores existed, so a route chosen from it is a selection made with the outcomes in view and must be disclosed as such wherever it is shown; it adds no metric and changes no decision of the entries on the artist lists. Counts from the script output are not a result of the models.
Limitation: right and wrong are shown per artist-year and do not measure the models; WHEN rows after the holdout (label_after_holdout) and the last data year are not counted as right or wrong.

## Demo: tab 6 mode 2, several artists in one year (19-10-2026)

Decision: tab 6 has two modes chosen by one radio control (game.mode_options of app/demo_config.yaml: "One artist" and "Several artists"); the first shown is game.default_mode, now "many" (several artists), and the one-artist mode of the entry "Demo: tab 6 flow and scoring (19-10-2026)" is one click away and unchanged. Mode 2 is a table with two steps and no question, no lock, no scoreboard and no game state of mode 1. On open, and after any change of the year or of the selected artists, the table shows the artist column and, in every other column, a placeholder cell (route.texts.placeholder, a dim "?" in a dark cell with a thin dashed border, drawn with rgba and currentColor so it follows a dark or a light theme, all placeholder cells of equal width), in the order of the selection so that the order does not give the scores away. The primary full-width button route.texts.predict_button, placed right above the table, shows the WHEN and WHAT model verdicts as rounded pills (the WHAT missing text where there is no WHAT score) and the rows sorted by the next-year WHEN score; the realities and the marks stay placeholders. The primary full-width button route.texts.reveal_button, offered only after the first one, shows the realities in plain text, the result marks and the two summary lines under the table. The two reliability lines, the legend and the cascade sentence are in an expander (route.texts.how_label), collapsed by default and shown from Predict on. The year, genre and artist pickers are inside a collapsed expander (route.texts.change_label); the prepared-selection note stays visible above the table. The stage is kept in st.session_state (route.keys.stage), is reset by a change of year or artists, and is not touched by a change in mode 1; the year, genre and artist widgets of both modes keep their values when the other mode is shown. The scores are the live scores of the saved models (Work rows) and the stored year-end scores (entry "Demo: WHEN scores for artist-years without a Work (20-10-2026)"), with no new model call.
Rows: when_rows of src/app_live.py returns, for an artist and a year, both WHEN horizons of the representative Work row when the artist has a Work that year, otherwise of the year-end row, otherwise none. An artist with no row is not in the table. One or two lines under the table say how many selected artists are left out, from the yaml and counted from the data (hidden_counts of src/demo_multi.py): before the last data year, "{n} selected artists have no earlier releases before {year}." (route.texts.no_history_one and no_history_many, singular for 1), for artists with no dated Work before the year; for the last data year (2026), "{n} selected artists have no release yet in {year}." (route.texts.no_release_one and no_release_many, singular for 1), for artists with earlier Works but no 2026 Work and so no row. When both cases occur in one table both lines show, the first one first.
Default view: year route.year (2019), route.max_artists 10 and the prepared selection route.artists (Discogs ids of SZA, Romeo Santos, Olivia Rodrigo, boygenius, Arctic Monkeys, Kacey Musgraves, Charli XCX, Dua Lipa, BTS and Labrinth), with one line above the table (route.selection_note) saying that the prepared selection was built for 2019, was chosen after seeing the model results to show both hits and misses, and is not a sample and not an evaluation. The selection was made from the table of the entries "Demo: route table for choosing a disclosed route (19-10-2026)" and "Demo: WHEN scores for artist-years without a Work (20-10-2026)", with the outcomes in view, and is disclosed as such on screen. Figures from the app functions for 2019, equal to results/demo/route_table_year_end.csv: next year right in 5 of 10, within 2 years right in 2 of 4 where shown, WHAT right in 3 of 6.
Table: one row per artist, sorted by the next-year WHEN score descending (ties by name) from the first step on. Columns: Artist, "Release next year?" (prediction and reality), "New style in next release?" (prediction and reality; header tooltip "Next release, whenever it comes. Independent of the first forecast.") and Result (no Release column); two header rows, no scores on screen. A year-end row carries, under the artist name, the small muted tag route.texts.year_end_tag ("No release in {year} · prediction from earlier releases"); a Work row shows nothing extra. A reality cell with a Yes reads "Yes: <title>", the title of the release of the year the verdict refers to (Y + 1 for Next year; for Within 2 years the year of the release, Y + 1 when the next-year label is Yes, else Y + 2), chosen by the entry "Demo: release title of the reality cells (20-10-2026)", cut at route.title_max_chars (28) with an ellipsis and the full title in the tooltip (title attribute); a Yes with no title in the file shows only "Yes". A row without a WHAT score shows one grey dash over the two WHAT columns, with the tooltip "No style prediction for this year" for a Work row that is not eligible and the tooltips of the entry "WHAT year-end rows (20-10-2026)" for a year-end row, and its grey dot mark the tooltip "No prediction"; a WHAT reality reads "Yes (<year>)" or "No (<year>)" with the year of the release it refers to (next_active_year of results/demo/live/what_labels.csv). WHEN cascade in one cell: a pill "Next year" first (Yes or No at game.threshold, 0.5, a filled accent pill for Yes and an outlined one for No); when its verdict is No, an arrow and a pill "Within {n} years" follow; when it is Yes nothing is added, unless the 2-year verdict is No, in which case both pills show. The reality cell has the same structure. WHAT model verdict: Likely as a filled pill and Not likely as an outlined pill, at the score_cutoff of results/holdout_what/top10_lift.csv (the top 10% rule of the entry "Demo: tab 6 flow and scoring (19-10-2026)"). Marks: one per pill shown, then the WHAT mark, in the same order, shared with tab 7 and the proof of concept: solid marks with a bold glyph (poc.colors.mark_green with a white check, mark_red with a white cross, each followed by an asterisk for a result shown for reference only, mark_grey for no reality; there is no amber), sizes and weight in poc.sizes (mark_size, mark_font, mark_weight, mark_pad); the asterisk rule is read from the stored flags (label_after_holdout and is_projection). The verdict pills use one accent color (poc.colors.accent), never green or red, which are kept for right and wrong. Every style rule of the table is scoped to the .poc-multi wrapper and every rule starts with .poc-, so no rule can reach another tab.
Summary under the table after the second step (route.texts.summary_when and summary_what): "Next year: k of N · Within 2 years (where shown): k of N" and the WHAT line, each as the model right over the rows with a counted reality (WHEN: label present and reality year 2025 or earlier, the rule of reality_status and is_counted of src/demo_game.py, the 2-year count only over rows where the second pill is shown; WHAT: label present, a Work row and next active year 2025 or earlier; the entry "Counting rule by reality year and the 2027 forecast (20-10-2026)" holds the rule). WHAT is never mixed into the WHEN count. The How to read this expander holds the two reliability lines of mode 1, the legend and "The 2-year view is shown when the next-year answer is No; a Yes next year also holds for 2 years." (route.texts.legend_cascade).
Manual choice: a year selector from years.holdout_start (2018) to the last data year of the Works file, a genre filter and a multiselect over the mixed list of both pools (sorted by name), capped at route.max_artists (10); a result whose reality year is 2026 shows the asterisk and is out of every count, and 2026 is shown as the forecast for 2027 (entry "Counting rule by reality year and the 2027 forecast (20-10-2026)").
Files: src/app_live.py (when_rows, load_tables), src/demo_multi.py (route_ids, picker_options, multi_rows, pick_title, reality_titles, md_escape, playable_years, play_year, reality_line, hidden_counts, hidden_lines, row_marks, multi_summary, reliability_lines), multi_table_html, reality_item, year_end_tag, short_title, cascade_div, cascade_cell, side_panel_html, multi_css, pill and how_lines in src/poc_view.py, render_multi and the mode 1 block in app/streamlit_app.py (widgets only), scripts/run_multi_apptest.py, scripts/run_game_sim.py, scripts/run_game_apptest.py, scripts/run_style_apptest.py and scripts/run_app_live_test.py.
Tests (print-only, 20-10-2026, all test scripts exit 0): the default view, the row order against an independent order from the stored csv files, the summary against an independent recount and against route_table_year_end.csv, boygenius with the colspan texts, years 2019, 2024 and 2026, manual picks from both pools with the cap, the switch keeping the mode 1 history, the three stages (placeholders, model columns without realities, everything), the structure (controls expander collapsed with the pickers inside, primary buttons, pills only yes or no, the notes location, only .poc-* style rules), the reset on a change of year or artists with the mode 2 stage untouched by mode 1, the year-end rows (tag, WHAT text, cascade pills only when the next year or the 2-year verdict is No, marks count), the hidden-artists lines for 2018 (plural), 2019 (singular) and 2026 (singular and plural, none of the "no earlier releases" text, both lines with stubbed counts because no table in the data has both cases); titles in the reality cells against results/demo/release_pick.csv, truncation with the tooltip (3 cut titles in the default view, BTS among them), no Release column, the tag under the artist of the year-end rows only, no session-state warning for the route_year selectbox (index is passed only at its first draw, with a positive control for the capture); mode 1 (scripts/run_game_apptest.py, scripts/run_game_sim.py): the playable years equal the route_table_year_end.csv years of all 61 artists, the cascade and the reality lines of Work and year-end years against that table, the scoreboard follows counted_1y (a reference-only year-end row leaves it unchanged) and the Work-year board equals the earlier walk; the tab 6 table after tab 7 in scripts/run_style_apptest.py.
Limitation: the table was not seen rendered in a browser; the prepared selection is a selection with the outcomes in view and its counts are not a result of the models; year-end scores are extrapolated and are tagged as prediction from earlier releases.

## Demo: tab 6 mode 1 layout: compact bar, windowed timeline, two-column panel (20-10-2026)

Decision: mode 1 (one artist) is rebuilt from the flow of the entry "Demo: tab 6 flow and scoring (19-10-2026)", with the proof of concept page merged in (the page app/poc_timeline.py and its test were deleted, see the entry "Tab 6 panel wording, independent Timing and Style questions, new styles in the WHAT reality (20-10-2026)"); the game state, widget keys, two-step flow (lock, prediction, reveal), counted_1y scoreboard and the route_year warning fix are unchanged, mode 2 is untouched. Top to bottom: (1) one compact bar on one line at wide widths (the page uses layout "wide"): genre, artist, the "My prediction" checkbox, the action button of the step (Lock my answer, Reveal reality, Next year; the yes or no choice sits next to the lock button), Restart, and under the bar one wrapping line of scoreboard chips (model, user, user vs model, session totals); (2) the question line of the current year; (3) a one-row timeline of 7 cards: poc.years_before 5 years, the current year and poc.years_after 1 year, with an "Earlier years hidden" marker when the window starts after 2010 (config.yaml demo_works_first_year); (4) three columns: the year with its representative Work (title on up to 2 lines, genres and styles only in the tooltip), the Timing block and the Style block of the entry "Tab 6 panel wording, independent Timing and Style questions, new styles in the WHAT reality (20-10-2026)"; (5) one closed expander "How to read this" with the reliability lines, the reference-only legend, the cascade sentence, the independence sentence and the notes; the unseen-artist line stays visible above the bar.
Cards: year, up to poc.titles_per_card (3) titles each on at most 2 lines with an ellipsis and the full title, genres and styles in the tooltip, a "+N more" line; no genres or styles are printed. A year without a dated Work shows the short grey text "No release" (poc.texts.gap_card), and a year-end year the grey tag route.texts.year_end_tag. The card of the predicted year (current year + 1) carries the WHEN and WHAT marks built from the mode 2 row (card_marks with row_marks, so year-end years work): grey dots before the reveal, green or red (asterisk when reference only) after it. All cards of the row have the same height (flex stretch, no fixed height, no overflow rule other than the 2-line ellipsis), long words wrap, and the card minimum width is poc.sizes.card_min_width 110, so 7 cards fit one row at 1280 and 1920 px (about 150 px and 245 px per card in the wide layout).
Removed: game.max_cards_per_year, scoreboard_title, what_yes, what_no, hidden_card, future_card, gap_card and the Discogs id caption (the id is no longer shown anywhere on screen in mode 1, mode 2 or tab 7, it stays internal for widget keys and joins; ui.id_caption is removed); the restart text is "Restart". Tests (print-only, 20-10-2026, scripts/run_game_apptest.py, run_game_sim.py, run_multi_apptest.py, run_style_apptest.py, run_app_live_test.py, all exit 0): window of 7 years (5 before, 1 after) and the marker rule, no genres or styles in the visible text of any card, bar widgets, one action button per step, panel content, one expander with the long lines, scoreboard follows counted_1y, in three states (ask, prediction, reveal) for A$AP Rocky and Taylor Swift.
Limitation: AppTest does not render CSS; the layout was checked in the running app with headless Firefox (Marionette) at 1280 and 1920 px, see the entry "Tab 6 panel wording, independent Timing and Style questions, new styles in the WHAT reality (20-10-2026)".

## Tab 6 panel wording, independent Timing and Style questions, new styles in the WHAT reality (20-10-2026)

Decision: the mode 1 panel is two question blocks, each with a small grey label from the config (poc.texts.label_timing "Timing", label_style "Style"), so they read as separate questions, and no score is on screen. Timing: "Will this artist drop a new work next year?", the user prediction line "Your prediction: Yes" (only when the checkbox is on, only in this block), "Prediction: Yes" (Yes or No pill), "Within 2 years: Yes" only when the existing cascade rule shows it (next-year verdict or 2-year verdict is No) with its own reality line, then "Reality: Yes" and, below it, "<title> (<year>)" (rule B, title cut at route.title_max_chars with the full title in the tooltip). Style: "Will the next release bring a new style?", an always visible small grey line "Next release, whenever it comes. Independent of the answer above. Compared with everything released up to <shown year>." (poc.texts.style_scope), "Prediction: Likely" or "Not likely" (verdict rule unchanged, top 10% cutoff), and "Reality: Yes, new style: <styles>" with the release title and year below, or "Reality: No new style" with the release title, its year and its styles followed by "(none new)". Before the reveal every Reality line shows "?" and nothing else. The year of the Style block belongs to the release: it is the next active year of the label and can differ from the Timing release. The score of each prediction word is its tooltip ("Model score 0.55, not a probability", poc.texts.score_tip), never a percentage, because the scores are not calibrated; the lines "Model score" and "Style score" are removed. Year-end rows keep the grey tag and the asterisk rules; a row without a WHAT score keeps the grey dash with its tooltip, and the Style grey line stays. The two predictions are independent: the grey line of the Style block and the tooltip of the style column say so (poc.texts.style_scope, route.texts.col_style_tip).
Mode 2 keeps the table: the two result headers are "Release next year?" and "New style in next release?" (header tooltip "Next release, whenever it comes. Independent of the first forecast."), each over a Prediction and a Reality column; same words (Likely, Not likely, Yes, No), the release title with its year on the reality cells, no scores.
New styles of the WHAT reality: a Work picked by rule B can hide the Work that carried the new style, so they are computed at artist-year level with the label rule of src/what_target.py (build_artist_years): new styles of (artist, Y) = styles of all dated Works of the next active year minus the vocabulary seen through Y (dated Works up to Y plus the styles of undated Works, which count as seen); for a year Y without a dated Work the result equals that of the last active year before it. results/demo/what_new_styles.csv (artist_id, year, next_active_year, new_styles as a JSON list) holds it for the 61 demo artists and every year from 2018 to 2026 from the full Works data, without a model; it is built by src/what_new_styles.py through scripts/run_what_new_styles.py with exclusive creation (a second run only repeats the control). 546 rows. Control: for every stored label of what_labels.csv and what_labels_b.csv (320 rows), label equals (new_styles is not empty): 0 label mismatches, 0 next_active_year mismatches, 0 stored labels without a row; the control is in the script output and in run_game_apptest.py and run_game_sim.py (320 labels, 152 yes). At most 3 style names are shown, then "+N" (poc.texts.max_styles); the names come from the data.
Layout fixes: the unseen-artist line is again one short visible line above the bar; the checkbox label is "My prediction" and the bar column is wide enough for it at 1280 and 1920 px (rendered in the running app with headless Firefox through Marionette: label not clipped, bar on one line, "Lock my answer" not clipped, 7 cards of equal height without overflow, no horizontal scroll, A$AP Rocky 2019 and 2021 in the three states); the scoreboard chips moved to one wrapping line under the bar because five chips do not fit beside the controls at 1280 px.
Deleted: the proof of concept page app/poc_timeline.py and scripts/run_poc_apptest.py (mode 1 covers them), and what only they used: the functions marks, table_rows, notes, panel_html, result_cell, side_panel_html, what_pill_html, na_html, demo_multi.reality_line and md_escape, the poc.keys block and the poc texts and colors of the old table and page (panel_title, col_score, col_prediction, col_reality, col_result, row_when, row_what, row_you, no_what_score, result_no_reality, result_no_what_reality, action_lock, action_reveal, action_next, restart, genre_label, artist_label, green, red, grey, page_title), and the game texts model_score, what_score, model_verdict, user_answer, when_title, what_title, reality_yes, reality_yes_title, reality_no, next2_info, label_part and no_reality of mode 1. Everything mode 1 and mode 2 use is kept.
Tests (print-only, 20-10-2026, all exit 0): the panel line order in the three states against the route table, the label file, the new styles file and the pick file, "?" before the reveal, no score text and no percentage in the panel, the WHAT words, the Within 2 years line only when the rule shows it, the grey Style line with the shown year, new styles for a Yes and "(none new)" for a No, the control above, the visible unseen-artist line (Olivia Rodrigo), the mode 2 headers and tooltip, the independence sentence in both expanders.

## Demo: release title of the reality cells (20-10-2026)

Decision (rule B): the reality cells of tab 6 (mode 1 and mode 2) show the title of the release that happened in the year the verdict refers to. When an artist-year has several Works, the title is the first album-like Work (is_album_like of data/processed/snapshots.parquet: Album, LP, EP, Mini-Album, entry "Work-type feature") in the seeded within-year order (works_so_far_this_year of the snapshots, the order of the entry "Feature point-in-time audit"); with no album-like Work, the representative Work (the last in that order). Re-recordings (is_rerecording, entry "Demo: re-recordings (17-10-2026)") are skipped; if the representative Work is a re-recording the last remaining Work is used, and an artist-year with only re-recordings gets no title (none occurs). The works files of the demo have no album marker, so src/release_pick.py takes it from the snapshots by work_id and writes results/demo/release_pick.csv (artist_id, year, work_id, title, is_album_like, picked_by) once, through scripts/run_release_pick.py (exclusive write, controls first). Coverage: every artist-year of both pools with a Work from the holdout start on, 2018 to 2026, so the years after the holdout and 2024 are included (430 artist-years, every next year of the route table that has a Work has a pick). Picks: 231 album, 199 fallback; among the 297 artist-years with several non-re-recording Works, 190 album and 107 fallback. A Yes with no title in the file shows only "Yes". Texts and the title limit: route.title_max_chars (28), game.reality_yes_title.
Evidence: the file was recomputed with plain loops in scripts/run_game_sim.py and is identical; the titles of all 521 rows of results/demo/route_table_year_end.csv (799 titles) equal the rule.
Limitation: Works not marked album-like include untagged formats (entry "Work-type feature": 35.2% other), so some albums are picked by the fallback; the Taylor Swift years with Taylor's Version re-recordings fall back to a remaining single or other Work.

## Demo: live style forecast for 2023 and later years (19-10-2026)

Decision: the demo gets a live forecast of the locked Random Forest for target year 2023 only. The model is refitted offline with LOCKED_RF_PARAMS and the project seed (src/style_forecast.py), on the training rows of the walk_forward procedure for T = 2023: origins at most 2021 with a known label, so every count it reads is dated 2022 or earlier; it predicts the styles eligible at origin 2022. The scores are live scores with no evaluation: they enter no metric, are not part of the test (the test of the entry "Style trend forecast, adoption and pass rule results, final test, 2024 projection and output procedure" stays target years 2018 to 2022) and no tuning or choice uses them. 2023 stays excluded from the test as in the design entry (anomalous, close to the loading lag). The ranges use the percentiles of the log residuals of the folds scored before 2023 (target years 1993 to 2022), the same pool as the stored 2024 projection row; no residual is recomputed from newer data.
No forecast for 2025 and 2026: a forecast needs the counts of the origin year (2024 and 2025) as inputs and, for the training rows, the labels of those years, and the entry "Style trend forecast, design fixed before any model result exists" excludes 2024 to 2026 everywhere (submission lag, partial year). The counts are also incomplete: styled Works per year are 228,396 (2023), 205,532 (2024), 182,108 (2025) and 87,324 (2026, partial year, dump dated 01-09-2026), source data/processed/works_checkpoint.parquet through style_table of src/style_forecast.py, and a model fed with them would read the cataloguing delay as a fall in growth. The app shows a text for these years and no model is fitted or scored.
Reality: the actual Works of each target year are stored in a small file (actuals.csv) read from the same style table; for 2018 to 2023 they are the c_next and the label of build_panel for the styles eligible at the origin before the target year, for 2024 the count of the Works dated 2024 of the styles of the 2024 projection. The 2024 reality is display only, flagged for cataloguing delay, and enters no training, tuning or metric. 2023 and 2024 are flagged, 2018 to 2022 are the final test years, 2025 and 2026 have no forecast and no reality.
Files and control (step output of 19-10-2026): src/live_style_extra.py (logic) and scripts/run_live_style_extra.py (calls it; refuses to run when any new file exists and writes with exclusive creation), no spent script run. New files in results/demo/live/style/: random_forest_2023.joblib 7,194.8 KB, panel_rows_extra.csv 58.6 KB (249 rows, the styles eligible at origin 2022), percentiles_extra.csv 0.1 KB (2 rows), actuals.csv 64.6 KB (1,693 rows, target years 2018 to 2024), control_extra.csv 1.0 KB (11 rows). Training rows 8,936 (origins at most 2021, label known); the per-year models total 41.5 MB against the cap of 150 MB. Residual percentiles of 2023: random_forest q10 -0.1711 and q90 0.1864, repeat_last q10 -0.1652 and q90 0.2074, equal to the stored 2024 row (maximum difference 5.6e-17). Runtime of the step 66 s.
Controls before saving (results/demo/live/style/control_extra.csv, all passed): the new model equals an independent walk_forward fit for target year 2023 (249 styles, maximum difference 0, limit 0.00005); the origin rows rebuilt from Works dated up to 2022 equal the full build (249 rows, maximum difference 0, limit 1e-9); the model columns equal columns.json (19); the hits at 10 per test year computed from actuals.csv and the stored forecast (6, 8, 5, 6, 5 for 2018 to 2022) equal the Random Forest column of results/style_forecast/test_20261015_173215.out; the actual Works equal an independent count from the style table (1,693 rows, difference 0); no (style, target_year) key is repeated; 249 forecast rows with ranks 1 to 249; reload check from the saved files, maximum difference 1.8e-12 against 0.00005; the SHA-256 of the 15 existing files under results/demo/live/style and results/style_forecast is identical before and after the run.
Limitations: the 2023 forecast is for an anomalous year (spikes in Noise Rock, Plunderphonics, Alt-Pop and Future House according to the design entry) and its misses are expected; the ranges of the predicted top 10 under-cover (entry "Style trend forecast, adoption and pass rule results"); 2024 counts are incomplete and the display only reality is therefore read with caution; no year after 2024 has a forecast.

## Demo: WHEN scores for artist-years without a Work (20-10-2026)

Option A (scoring artist-years with no dated Work) had no entry under PENDING, so it is recorded here directly as DECIDED. Files: src/when_year_end.py (logic) and scripts/run_when_year_end.py (calls it; refuses to run when any of the four new files exists and writes with exclusive creation). New files: results/demo/live/when_features_year_end.csv 9.5 KB (91 rows, keys artist_id, snapshot_year, work_id empty, then the 20 model columns), results/demo/when_demo_scores_year_end.csv 9.8 KB (182 rows, the layout of results/demo/when_demo_scores.csv plus is_year_end, true on every row), results/demo/route_table_year_end.csv 54.9 KB (521 rows: the 430 representative rows of results/demo/route_table.csv plus the 91 year-end rows), results/demo/live/control_when_year_end.csv 1.1 KB. Runtime of the step 2 s.
Definition: a year-end snapshot exists for a demo artist (61 artists, both pools) and a year Y from 2018 to the last complete year (2025) when the artist has no dated Work in Y and at least one dated Work before Y. No row for 2026 (partial year, the dump is dated 01-09-2026) and none before the first Work. Result: 91 rows for 39 artists (pool A 36, pool B 55); per year 2018 7, 2019 6, 2020 10, 2021 11, 2022 11, 2023 13, 2024 16, 2025 17.
Features (point in time, only Works dated before Y): works_so_far_this_year, albums_so_far_this_year, singles_so_far_this_year, is_album_like and is_single_like are 0 (both type flags false means other); history_count is the number of dated Works up to Y; years_since_last is Y minus the year of the last Work; years_active_so_far is Y minus the first Work year; historical_rate is history_count divided by years_active_so_far (never missing here, because the first Work is before Y). These are the values the snapshot of the first Work of year Y holds in build_snapshots of src/snapshots.py. build_snapshots itself cannot build the row (it creates one row per Work), so the same definitions are applied in year_end_rows of src/when_year_end.py and checked against it. Genre columns come from the genres of the artist's last Work before Y in the snapshot sort order, passed through build_features of src/models.py as for any snapshot (the snapshot of a Work uses that Work's own genres, so the year-end row uses the nearest Work in the past; the genre set equals the genre set of the first-Work snapshot of the same year in 34.7% of the 389 reproduction artist-years, an expected difference of definition and not a check).
Targets (reality): next_1y and next_2y by the rule of build_snapshots (next active year after Y within 1 or 2 years gives 1 or 0; with no later active year the value is 0 when enough fully observed years follow and missing otherwise). is_projection is true when the label is missing; label_after_holdout is true when Y is after years.holdout_end (2023); artist_train_rows is the number of training rows of the artist in the holdout run, as in demo_scores of src/when_demo.py. The reality counts in the route table under the rule of is_counted of src/demo_game.py applied to each horizon (label present, not projection, not after the holdout): 58 of 91 rows (63.7%) for both horizons; a label exists on 89.0% (next_1y) and 85.7% (next_2y) of the rows.
Model: the saved results/demo/live/when_next_1y.json and when_next_2y.json with the column lists of results/demo/live/columns.json; nothing is refitted and no evaluation is run. results/demo/live_scores.csv is neither read nor hashed.
Controls before writing (results/demo/live/control_when_year_end.csv, all passed): the SHA-256 of the 36 existing files under results/demo (live folder included) is identical before and after the run; point in time: rows rebuilt with the Works after the cut removed equal the full build for cuts 2022, 2023, 2024 and 2025 (45, 58, 74 and 91 rows, maximum difference 0); reproduction: for 389 artist-years that do have Works, with that year's Works removed from the table, the rebuilt history_count, years_since_last, years_active_so_far, historical_rate, next_1y and next_2y equal those of the existing snapshot with works_so_far_this_year equal to 1 (maximum difference 0, same missing positions); the model columns equal columns.json (20 per model); no missing or infinite model input (91 rows by 20 columns); scores between 0.2778 and 0.9211; no repeated (artist, year); the 430 existing route rows rebuilt from the stored score files equal the 1-year verdict and reality of results/demo/route_table.csv.
Figures (step output, not a result of the models): Yes verdicts at 0.5, next_1y 68 of 91 (74.7%) and next_2y 88 of 91 (96.7%); 20 rows with next_1y No and next_2y Yes. Route table: 50 one-year No verdicts (23 year-end), of which 8 right of 32 with a counted reality; 39 of the 50 have a 2-year Yes (19 right of 23 counted) and 11 a 2-year No (0 right of 9 counted).
Limitation: the models were trained on rows with works_so_far_this_year of at least 1, so these rows are an extrapolation outside the training support; they were not trained or evaluated on such rows, they enter no metric, and the holdout is spent, so no new evaluation is made. The scores are shown with that label. The near-constant Yes at the 2-year horizon (96.7%) shows that the extrapolated 2-year score carries little information. The route table was built with the outcomes in view and chooses no route. WHAT for these rows is in the entry "WHAT year-end rows (20-10-2026)".

## WHAT year-end rows (20-10-2026)

Decision: artist-years without a dated Work get a reference-only WHAT score, built from the last active year L. Files: src/what_year_end.py (logic, a thin wrapper over build_artist_years and build_what_features of src/what_target.py, which are not edited) and scripts/run_what_year_end.py (run once, refuses to run when any of the four new files exists, exclusive creation). New files: results/demo/live/what_features_year_end.csv, results/demo/live/what_labels_year_end.csv, results/demo/live/control_what_year_end.csv, results/demo/what_scores_year_end.csv (paths in app/demo_config.yaml, live.*). Scope: the 61 demo artists, gap years Y from 2018 to 2025 (the range of the WHEN year-end rows) with last active year L before Y and at least 3 dated Works strictly before Y (the WHAT minimum, config what.min_previous_works). 87 rows; per year 2018 6, 2019 5, 2020 10, 2021 10, 2022 10, 2023 13, 2024 16, 2025 17.
Encoding rule: the row is the L row of the WHAT features with the same-year columns set to 0, not NaN: n_works_year, n_styles_year, n_new_styles_year, albums_share_year, singles_share_year and every genre_* column; years_since_prev_active_year is Y minus L, years_active_so_far is Y minus the first active year, history_count is the number of dated Works before Y, years_since_new_style is the value of L plus (Y minus L) (a missing value stays missing); everything else is copied from L. No dummy Works are created.
Label: copied from the L row with the existing rules (label_table of src/live_what_extended.py: label_after_holdout is true when the label exists and Y or the next active year is after 2023). It is not independent evidence: the gap-row label is the label of L, so several gap years of one artist share one outcome, and the label of L is the same fact already counted for the Work row of L. Rows: 87, of which 22 without a resolved label (no later release in the data yet) and 35 flagged after the holdout.
Reference only: the saved WHAT stage 0 model and results/demo/live/columns.json are used with no refit; the verdict uses the top 10% cutoff 0.4943 of results/holdout_what/top10_lift.csv. Every year-end WHAT row is shown with a plain green or red mark (no asterisk), is never counted (what_counted false, so it is outside the WHAT count of mode 2, because its label copies the one of the last active year and counting it would duplicate an outcome) and no right or wrong is stored. The WHAT holdout is spent: no accuracy figure of any kind is computed, stored or printed for these rows. The horizon is variable: the next active year of L can be 1 to several years after Y, unlike the fixed next-year horizon of WHEN.
Scores: minimum 0.1121, median 0.3383, maximum 0.6289; Yes-share at 0.4943 is 3.4% overall (2018 0%, 2019 0%, 2020 10%, 2021 10%, 2022 0%, 2023 0%, 2024 0%, 2025 5.9%). Sensitivity (step output, not a result): with NaN instead of 0 for albums_share_year and singles_share_year the correlation with the chosen scores is 0.983 and the Yes-share 2.3% against 3.4%; with NaN also for n_styles_year and n_new_styles_year the correlation is 0.901, the Yes-share 0.0% and the median score 0.21 (against 0.34).
Controls before writing (results/demo/live/control_what_year_end.csv, all passed): point in time, rows rebuilt with the Works after 2022 and after 2024 removed equal the full build (41 and 70 rows, maximum difference 0); gap-row labels equal the labels of their L rows recomputed with plain sets (87 rows, 0 differ); the new styles of results/demo/what_new_styles.csv agree with the label (label equals new styles not empty) on 58 labelled L rows, 0 differ; model columns equal columns.json (25); no infinite value (one missing cell, years_since_new_style); scores between 0 and 1; no repeated key; normal-year scores recomputed in the same run reproduce stored stage0_score (160 rows, maximum difference 2.8e-08, limit 5e-5); the SHA-256 of 50 files under results/demo, results/demo/live and results/holdout_what is equal before and after (results/demo/live_scores.csv neither read nor hashed).
App: src/app_live.py loads the scores (live.what_scores_year_end); year_end_what of src/demo_multi.py fills the WHAT fields for both modes through multi_rows. A row with a score and a resolved label shows the verdict, the plain mark and the Style reality (new styles of L from results/demo/what_new_styles.csv, release title with the next active year). A row with a stored score and no resolved label (the 22 rows) shows the verdict, the grey dot mark and, as the Style reality line, "No later release in the data yet" (poc.texts.what_na_tip_no_later). The grey dash with the tooltip "Needs at least {n} earlier releases" (poc.texts.what_na_tip_needs_three) is only for a row without a stored score (fewer than 3 earlier Works). The "How to read this" expander of both modes has one more sentence (route.texts.year_end_what_note): style forecasts for years without a release are not counted. In the 2019 route Arctic Monkeys and Kacey Musgraves have a year-end WHAT score and boygenius has none; SZA and Olivia Rodrigo have a Work in 2019, so their rows are Work rows and not year-end rows. Tests: scripts/run_multi_apptest.py (check 14), scripts/run_game_apptest.py (reference from the new file), scripts/run_app_live_test.py, all exit 0.

## Counting rule by reality year and the 2027 forecast (20-10-2026)

Decision: a result counts when its reality year is 2025 or earlier (years.last_complete), and a result whose reality year is 2026 is shown with the asterisk and is not counted. The rule lives once in reality_status of src/demo_game.py (LAST_COUNTED); is_counted, horizon_of and horizons_of of src/app_live.py and multi_rows of src/demo_multi.py call it, and the stored flags is_projection and label_after_holdout are no longer read by tab 6 (the stored files, results/demo/route_table_year_end.csv and the counted_1y columns, are not recomputed or rewritten and keep the old rule). Reality years: next_1y is year + 1; the within-2-years item is year + 1 when the next-year label is Yes, else year + 2 (the release the cell shows); WHAT is next_active_year of results/demo/what_new_styles.csv. Applies to mode 1, mode 2 and the scoreboard; tab 7 is not touched.
Evidence (descriptive, not a model evaluation): the 61 demo artists have 155, 136, 133, 140, 139 and 153 Works (non re-recordings) in 2020 to 2025 with 51, 50, 50, 48, 45 and 44 active artists, so their catalogue looks complete up to 2025; 2026 has 78 Works up to the dump of 01-09-2026 and the year is not over. Counts over the demo artists, 2018 to 2026, both WHEN horizons: 547 counted, 53 with the asterisk, 86 without a reality.
WHAT of year-end rows: never counted and no asterisk, because their label is a copy of the last active year label and counting it would duplicate an outcome; the plain green or red mark stays. WHEN year-end rows follow the rule above. The WHAT summary reads "Style of the next release: model right in {k} of {n} years with a release." and "How to read this" says style forecasts for years without a release are not counted. Year-end rows have no asterisk; their grey tag stays.
Texts: poc.texts.legend_open_year "* {year} is not over yet, so this result can still change" ({year} from the data) is the legend (under the table in mode 2 and under the scoreboard chips in mode 1, only when a shown result has an asterisk, and never in an expander) and the tooltip of every asterisk mark. The 2019 route is unchanged by the rule: next year 5 of 10, within 2 years 2 of 4, WHAT 3 of 6 before and after; pool A pins of run_game_sim.py moved from 5/3, 6/6 and 6/6 to 6/3, 7/7 and 7/6 counted years and model right (2024 now counts).
Forecast for 2027: a row of the last data year (2026) has no reality. Mode 1 and mode 2 show the title route.texts.forecast_title "Forecast for 2027" and the line forecast_note "2027 has not happened yet, so there is no reality to compare." above the panel or table, no reveal step (mode 2 offers only Predict; mode 1 goes from the prediction to Next, Restart unchanged), reality cells read route.texts.not_yet "Not yet" with no title, no mark and no asterisk, nothing is counted and the scoreboard is unchanged. Artists without a 2026 Work have no row (the 2026 year-end rows stay excluded) and are listed by the existing "no release yet in 2026" line.
Route for 2026: route.artists_by_year[2026] (pick_route of src/demo_multi.py, route_for_year switches the selection when the year changes and restores the default list when it goes back). The 2019 route was chosen by hand with the outcomes in view and has no code rule, so the 2026 rule keeps only its shape: route.mix_yes (6) Yes verdicts and 4 No verdicts at the 0.5 threshold among the artists with a 2026 Work, each group in ascending Discogs id; only 2 of the 36 artists with a 2026 Work have a No verdict, so the list has 8 Yes and 2 No (Mary J. Blige, Shakira, Eminem, Jennifer Lopez, Beyonce, Drake, Bruce Springsteen, Calvin Harris, Arctic Monkeys, SZA). No reality exists, so no outcome was in view. route.texts.selection_note_by_year[2026] discloses it.
Tests (print-only, all exit 0): run_multi_apptest.py (reality 2025 counted without asterisk against an independent recount, 2025 rows with the asterisk and nothing counted, the 2026 title, no reveal, "Not yet" cells, the prepared list equal to the rule), run_game_apptest.py, run_game_sim.py and run_app_live_test.py (counted and starred horizons by reality year, WHAT year-end rows never counted and without asterisk), run_style_apptest.py (exit 0, unchanged).

## Text rules for the audience (20-10-2026)

Decision: every text on screen follows six rules. One word per concept: "release" (never Work, work or album; re-recording stays), "prediction" for a Yes or No about an artist and "forecast" only for tab 7 and the 2027 block, "Reality" for what happened (never actual, real or result as a noun), the two questions are "Timing" and "Style" (WHEN and WHAT never appear on screen), "Not counted" for excluded rows, and "tested on {years}" for the evaluation. Every number inside a text comes from the config or the data through a placeholder (the second horizon game.second_horizon, the reliability scale game.reliability_scale, the minimum of earlier releases config.what.min_previous_works, the open year from years.last_complete + 1, the tab 7 figures from the live forecast and the stored actuals). Each idea is said once on screen: the Timing question only in its block, the independence of the two questions only in the Style grey line and the style column tooltip, the asterisk only in the legend line near the chips or under the table and in its tooltip (tab 7: the reason of the year). The recommendation to prefer repeating last year (entry "Demo: tab 7 shows the Random Forest (19-10-2026)") stays in the decisions and is not on screen. The tab "EDA" is titled "Data exploration". Dead keys ui.empty_rows, notices.projection_notice, the route year and genre labels, route.texts.no_reality, route.texts.independent_note and poc.texts.legend_reference are removed.
Tests: scripts/run_multi_apptest.py, run_game_apptest.py, run_game_sim.py, run_app_live_test.py and run_style_apptest.py exit 0 with the new texts.

## PENDING
- Demo artist selection for the Streamlit Time Machine app: once snapshots_df exists, mine candidate artists algorithmically rather than picking by hand only: artists with the largest year_gap between consecutive works (hiatus/comeback candidates, good for demoing WHEN) and artists with the largest style-set discontinuity between consecutive works (genre-pivot candidates, good for demoing WHAT). Cross-reference with recognizable/well-known artists where possible, since a judge/recruiter audience responds better to a name they know than an equally-clean but obscure example.
- Album covers and song previews for the artist timeline in the demo: not available from the bulk XML dump (no image/video fields there). Available via the separate Discogs REST API, per release: images[].uri for cover art, videos[].uri for (usually YouTube) links Discogs itself associates with that release. Only needed for the small number of hand/algorithmically-selected demo artists, not the full dataset, so the API's rate limit (60 req/min unauthenticated, 240/min with a free token) is not a constraint. Embed the YouTube link via iframe in Streamlit (legal, no audio downloading) rather than hosting audio files directly. Not implemented yet.
- Modeling extras for the rubric (not part of the required 4-model comparison): add Naive Bayes as a 5th, lightweight model for diversity (expected weaker than tree/boosting models given correlated features, but cheap to include); add a stacking ensemble (meta-model trained on the base models' out-of-fold predictions) combining whichever models end up performing best, to explicitly hit the rubric's "ensembles" concept beyond RF/XGBoost's own internal ensembling. Consider PCA as a preprocessing step specifically for KNN if the final WHAT feature set ends up high-dimensional (e.g. one-hot genre/style columns), since KNN degrades with many dimensions; not applied to the other models to avoid losing interpretability. Update 15-10-2026: Naive Bayes was added as the 5th model and compared (DONE, see the entry "Naive Bayes, untuned" and notebooks/05_what_models.ipynb). Stacking and PCA are not done.
